# CineGlobe Four-Project Scenario and Project Globe Integrity Audit — Codex

Audit date: 2026-09-03  
Audit baseline: `9633e8d5b35472d1c6a88c6544fa00d36b44382a` (`canonical-1.52.0`)  
Mode: read-only forensic acceptance audit; no application or database remediation

## 1. Executive verdict

**NOT ACCEPTED.** The bounded acceptance universe is 1,336 current-fingerprint structures across four productions. The source budgets and most single-jurisdiction arithmetic are stable, incentive dollars do not exceed the served qualifying base, registered dollar caps do not breach, and every unpriced structure has null incentive/NPC. However, four P0 defect classes and five P1 defect classes remain.

The largest failures are systematic rather than jurisdiction-specific:

1. 26 component structures are served as `PRICED` even though their own mandatory `ELIGIBILITY` minimum-spend evaluation is `FAILED`.
2. 488 component structures persist adjusted NPC into both `true_net_cost_usd` and `risk_adjusted_net_cost_usd`, while their trace contains the correct pre-normalization NPC but omits the normalization fields. The API consequently labels adjusted NPC as `npc_verified_usd`, making the served scenario impossible to reconcile from its served fields.
3. The API drops the routed/partner jurisdiction from all 740 component structures and all 96 treaty structures (836 structures total), reducing `participants` to the primary jurisdiction. Scenario titles, flags, selection, Globe paths, and Inspector routing therefore lose the structure's defining second territory.
4. Saudi's official repository record says **up to 60%**, discretionary, preapproved, and content-cleared, but the executable rate tier is a flat deterministic 60% with `is_band_ceiling=false`; component candidates also lose the administrative-risk warning and can price below the statutory minimum-spend floor.

The Globe is not a scenario-complete projection. It reduces 1,336 scenarios to 89 point identities per project, 20 status-bearing jurisdictions per project have no coordinate/hit target, and the current Lips Like Sugar Australia point hovers the PDV scenario but clicks the Location Offset scenario. The reported Manitoba→Cyprus mismatch is **not reproducible on the audited baseline**: Manitoba currently resolves `CA-MB → Manitoba → CA-MB` for point, hover, and click, and there is no current `CA-MB → CY` mapping.

## 3. Economic acceptance matrix

| Invariant | Result | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| 1. Source budget is canonical and scenario-independent | PASS | Every non-null structure gross equals its project's declared gross; Overview, Workspace, Scenarios, Reports, and the API read `production.gross_budget_usd` / `structure.gross_budget_usd`. |
| 2. Program cap is not substituted for project incentive | PASS on current scenario economics; prior Overview opportunity display fixed | All priced incentives are calculated values and remain ≤ QPE; 733 capped segments have zero cap breach. The former summed-fund-cap display is absent at the audited baseline. |
| 3. QPE doctrine matches represented calculation base | FAIL | 26 priced component segments carry an explicit failed minimum-spend eligibility evaluation. Four invalid Ontario multi-program rows duplicate the same QPE across two program segments, but they are unpriced and the UI does not display their summed QPE. |
| 4. Incentive cannot exceed calculation base | PASS | Zero priced structures have `selected_incentive_usd > sum(segment.qpe_usd)`. |
| 5. NPC reproducible from canonical inputs | FAIL | 488 component API records label normalized NPC as verified NPC while serving all normalization deltas as null/zero. Hidden deltas range $10,340–$753,340 depending on project. |
| 6. Conditional/discretionary/preapproval status persists | FAIL | Full-relocation Saudi warnings reach Hero/Overview/Workspace, but not Scenarios, Globe hover, or Reports; Saudi component warnings are absent entirely. |
| 7. Ranking does not convert conditional to executable | PARTIAL PASS | Failed/conditional component rows have no numeric rank and no rank-1 row; nevertheless they are still `PRICED`, visible as viable economics, and selectable as leading. |
| 8. Same scenario identity/value/status everywhere | FAIL | API participant loss, component NPC field collapse, report fallback error, and Globe many-to-one reduction create cross-surface disagreement. |
| 9. No stale/duplicate calculation path feeds a UI | PASS for current generation | All project-scoped screens call the same `GET /cineglobe/projects/{id}/state`; the view is fingerprint-pinned. Historical row accumulation does not enter the served view. |
| 10. Country/province/state identity is correct | FAIL | Current Manitoba naming is correct, but 836 multi-territory structures lose their second jurisdiction, 20 mapped jurisdictions/project lack hit targets, and LLS Australia hover/click identities differ. |

## 4. Scenario calculation traces

The canonical chain is:

`BudgetDocument.total_budget_raw → BudgetLineItem → build_project_economic_inputs(read_only=True) → current input fingerprint → ProductionStructure + StructureCalculationResult → canonical_production_view → GET state → useCineGlobe → screen consumer`.

For ordinary single-country/full-relocation pricing, the intended formulas are:

`selected incentive = Σ segment confirmed/best-supported incentive`  
`verified NPC = gross − selected incentive + off-budget financing + implementation`  
`adjusted NPC = verified NPC + travel + FX + in-kind replacement + local-cost normalization`.

Single-country/full-relocation rows preserve the split. Component persistence does not: `canonical_evaluation.py` assigns `npc = pricing.npc_with_adjustments_usd` and writes that same value into both `true_net_cost_usd` and `risk_adjusted_net_cost_usd`; the component trace writes `pricing.npc_verified_usd` but omits `adjustments`. `canonical_production_view.py` then exposes the result column as `npc_verified_usd` and zero/default adjustments. Headline adjusted NPC is generally the calculator's adjusted total, but the trace and labels are false/incomplete.

Failed minimum-spend eligibility is also explicitly computed but deliberately not consumed. `allocation_pricing.py` calls `evaluate_requirements_gate()`, emits `FAILED`, then proceeds to rate/incentive calculation. The code comment says enforcement was deferred because segment-vs-production scope was unsettled. That is not acceptance-safe: a failed mandatory gate cannot coexist with `PRICED` without resolving the base definition or classifying the outcome conditional/unavailable.

The complete scenario-by-scenario trace/result matrix is in section 7.

## 5. Saudi 60% provenance/status finding

**Classification: CONDITIONAL.** The repository's current primary-source record supports a published **up to 60%** maximum effective May 2026, not a guaranteed flat award for every qualifying production.

Current path:

- `SA_DOCTRINE` is `VERIFIED`, with one tier `sa-flat-60`, `rate=0.60`, `is_band_ceiling=False`.
- `ProgramRequirementsProfile` records local entity/Saudi partner, preapproval, discretionary allocation, SAR 750,000 feature minimum (~$200,000), five shoot days, refundability, non-transferability, script content clearance, and a filming non-objection certificate.
- The profile itself says award criteria and whether 60% varies by expenditure category remain undisclosed.
- Because the rate tier is not a band ceiling, `ceiling_requires_confirmation=false`; full-relocation structures price 60% of QPE as selected incentive.
- Full-relocation warning propagation now reaches Hero, Overview cards, and Workspace cards. It does not reach Scenarios, Project Globe hover, or Reports.
- Component generation does not append `_competitive_allocation_disclosure`; component candidates therefore lose the warning. Ten Saudi component scenarios across the four projects exist; eight are priced despite failing the Saudi $200,000 minimum.
- Bankability is represented only indirectly (`refundable=true`, `transferable=false`, administrative warning). No project-specific approval, award letter, bridge-lender eligibility, or financing certainty is on file.

Current project-level exclusion integration boundary: the exclusion must be applied before candidate selection/ranking and consistently filter or classify the same structure IDs in API `allocated_structures`, Hero/best-priced fallback, Overview option selection, Workspace lanes/overflow/leading selection, Scenarios comparable/review selection, Globe status/points/arcs/Inspector routing, FX top-priced slot, and Reports. A UI-only toggle on one surface would leave contradictory consumers.

## 6. Cross-page consistency findings

- All project screens use `useCineGlobe(projectId)` and the same state endpoint. The frontend does not recompute incentive or NPC.
- Overview, Workspace, Scenarios, and Project Globe all consume `allocated_structures`, but each selects a subset differently. This is presentation selection, not a second evaluator.
- Hero/Overview can fall back to `bestPricedCandidate` when no rank-1 exists. Reports cannot: it looks only for `ranking.rank===1`, so Little Utopia and FVD report “No structure is fully priced yet” despite 250 and 320 fully priced structures respectively.
- Scenarios and Workspace calculate displayed QPE as a raw sum of segment QPE. That is valid for disjoint jurisdiction segments, but unsafe for same-base multi-program rows; current duplicated Ontario rows are unpriced, so the false sum is presently suppressed.
- Scenario display identity derives from `participants`. Because the API reduces component/treaty participants to the primary only, Overview/Workspace/Scenarios collapse distinct routed scenarios into indistinguishable primary-jurisdiction titles/flags. Internal labels still contain the routed destination, causing Project Globe's card list to disagree with shared scenario cards.

## 7. Complete Globe acceptance matrix

Matrix method: every current-fingerprint structure was evaluated. `EXPECTED ON GLOBE=YES` because all five current structure types carry at least one real jurisdiction; no current structure is intentionally non-geographic. `ACTUAL` means a unique point payload resolves to that exact structure ID. A shared polygon or a marker owned by another same-jurisdiction scenario is not accepted as scenario representation.

Status totals: **1,336 rows; 354 PASS; 982 FAIL.** Defect flags are cumulative.

## 8. Manitoba→Cyprus root cause

**Current result: DISPROVEN / historical cause unresolved.** On all four current views, Manitoba is `CA-MB`, `globeKey(CA-MB)=CA-MB`, coordinate name `Manitoba`, point ID/ISO `CA-MB`, and hover/click resolve the same Manitoba structure. No code path or data row maps `CA-MB` to `CY`, and current keying is not array-index based.

The earlier mismatch cannot be honestly attributed from the current baseline. What is proven is that the current generic identity mechanism can still disagree for a different jurisdiction: on Lips Like Sugar, the Australia point's hover payload is the Australia PDV Offset structure, while click uses `structuresByCode.get("AU")[0]`, the Australia Location Offset structure. This occurs because hover uses the status reducer's winning `entry.best`, while click independently chooses the first structure stored under the jurisdiction code. Manitoba currently happens to have the same object in both positions; Australia does not.

## 9. Non-hovering marker root cause(s)

**STATIC VERIFIED:** `buildCountryPoints()` skips every status-bearing jurisdiction absent from `JURISDICTION_COORDS`. Globe3D creates CSS2D `.globe-hit-target` elements only from that point array. Therefore 20 current status-bearing jurisdictions per project have polygon/status data but no hover/click target. The set is `CA-SK, CZ, IN, LU, MK, MN, PE, RS, SK, US-AZ, US-CO, US-NM, US-NV, US-OK, US-PA, US-TN, US-UT, UY, UZ, KZ`.

This includes Bad Hombres' rank-1 New Mexico base: the gold `US-NM` admin-1 polygon exists, but no `US-NM` coordinate exists, so the recommendation has no hit target. Three Australian subnational candidates (`AU-NSW`, `AU-QLD`, `AU-SA`) and Dubai (`AE-DXB`) additionally exist only as dropped component/treaty destinations and never enter status/point construction at all despite having coordinates.

For points that are created, hit testing is correctly implemented as a 28×28 CSS2D target with pointer events, z-index 45, keyboard activation, far-side `display:none`, and mount-level hover clearing. No evidence supports raycast, marker-size, or z-order as the systematic cause. Far-side targets are intentionally inert until rotation exposes them.

## 10. Missing-scenario Globe wiring findings

- Project Globe receives the entire API `allocated_structures.structures` array, but `buildCountryStatuses()` reduces it to one `best` structure per `globeKey`, then `buildCountryPoints()` emits one point per surviving jurisdiction.
- Each project has 89 points for 292–386 structures. Across the complete matrix, 982 scenarios fail unique Globe correspondence.
- Status maps contain 109 jurisdictions/project; 20 lack coordinates/hit targets. The additional expected routed destinations are lost before status construction because API `participants` is incomplete.
- No explicit “intentionally not represented” structure class exists. The loss is an implementation collapse, not a documented product decision.

## 11. Multi-jurisdiction representation findings

All 740 component and 96 treaty structures are affected. `_empty_structure_entry()` sets `participants` to `[primary_code]` regardless of trace `component_allocations`, `coproduction_partners`, or persisted `jurisdiction_allocations`. Consequences:

- anchor + post/VFX/music destination collapses to anchor only;
- treaty/co-production partner collapses to primary only;
- shared `scenarioDisplay()` and `compactScenarioIdentity()` omit the defining destination/partner;
- Globe cannot color or mark the second territory from that structure;
- optimizer overlay sees a single participant and produces no route;
- Project Globe card selection's `routedTo` falls back to primary;
- Inspector selection commonly opens the primary segment rather than the routed component.

All four jurisdiction-mode Globe views produce zero arcs at default state. This is expected only when no current active multi-jurisdiction structure exists; here it is also structurally guaranteed by the participant loss.

## 12. Canonical identity/keying findings

- Structure rows are UUID-keyed and unique within each current generation: zero duplicate structure IDs.
- Globe geography is keyed by `globeKey(jurisdiction_code)`, preserving US/Canada admin-1 codes; it is not keyed by array index or render order.
- Hover identity is `statuses[globeKey].best.structure_id`.
- Click identity is `structuresByCode[jurisdictionCode][0].structure_id`.
- Because those are independent selectors, one point can hover and click different structures (proven for LLS/Australia).
- Many distinct scenario IDs share the same participant-key and geography. The largest single participant-key collision is 169 LU, 241 FVD, 168 Bad Hombres, and 262 LLS structures, driven by primary-only component/treaty serialization.
- Manitoba is correctly preserved as a subnational identity at the audited baseline.

## 13. Evidence classification

**RUNTIME VERIFIED**

- FVD Overview loaded against the current local backend: budget $4,517,687; Saudi Top Priced Candidate; $2,628,204 NPC; discretionary/preapproval warning; same Saudi values on Top Structures and Budget card.
- Current state API returned all four projects and current-fingerprint structures.

**STATIC + CURRENT-DATA VERIFIED**

- All 1,336 scenario formula/status checks.
- Complete Globe reducer/point/hover/click matrix through production `buildGlobeView()`.
- Manitoba identity, LLS Australia hover/click mismatch, missing-coordinate set, participant loss, and Reports fallback.

**BLOCKED (environment, not counted as product defects)**

- A subsequent all-page browser sweep overwhelmed the already-running duplicated local backend processes; a separate read-only server was inaccessible cross-origin from the temporary frontend. Failed loads were not used as defect evidence.
- Exhaustive physical pointer hover/click of every far-side rotating CSS2D target was not technically practical. Every generated point payload and both event selection functions were exhaustively evaluated instead; one representative runtime Overview was exercised.

## 14. Exact P0 defects

1. **P0 — Mandatory eligibility failure priced:** 26 component structures are `PRICED` with non-null incentive/NPC while their own `requirement_trace` says minimum spend `FAILED` (LU 6, FVD 9, Bad Hombres 6, LLS 5).
2. **P0 — Component NPC trace/field corruption:** 488 component rows collapse normalized NPC into the verified column and omit the adjustment trace, preventing reproduction and mislabeling risk-normalized economics.
3. **P0 — Multi-jurisdiction identity destroyed at API boundary:** all 836 component/treaty structures lose their routed/partner jurisdiction in `participants`, corrupting identity and every downstream geographic/selection consumer.
4. **P0 — Saudi maximum modeled as guaranteed rate:** canonical evidence says “up to 60%” and discretionary, but the executable tier is deterministic flat 60%; component candidates also omit the warning and eight price below the Saudi minimum.

## 15. Exact P1 defects

1. **P1 — Globe is not scenario-complete:** 982/1,336 scenario rows lack one-to-one accepted Globe representation; one point per jurisdiction silently replaces scenario identity.
2. **P1 — Status-bearing jurisdictions are non-interactive:** 20 per project have no coordinate/hit target; Bad Hombres' rank-1 New Mexico is included.
3. **P1 — Hover/click selector divergence:** LLS Australia hovers PDV Offset but clicks Location Offset (`entry.best` versus first-by-code).
4. **P1 — Reports false empty-state:** LU and FVD say no structure is fully priced because Reports requires rank 1, despite 250/320 priced structures.
5. **P1 — Conditional status is not cross-surface complete:** Saudi administrative risk is absent from Scenarios, Globe hover, Reports, and all component warnings.

## 16. Recommended one consolidated remediation boundary

One bounded remediation should repair the **canonical scenario DTO and its consumers**, not patch countries or screens independently:

1. Enforce mandatory eligibility in `price_segment` using an explicit canonical base-scope rule; failed gates cannot produce priced incentives.
2. Persist component verified/adjusted NPC and every adjustment exactly as ordinary candidates do.
3. Build `participants` from persisted allocations + component/treaty trace and expose one canonical `scenario_identity`/`geographies` object.
4. Represent published rate maxima as conditional ceilings unless a project fact confirms the award.
5. Make Globe points carry a scenario ID (or an explicit scenario group with selectable members); hover and click must consume the same resolved object.
6. Complete coordinates/hit targets for every executable jurisdiction or disclose `BLOCKED_NO_GEOMETRY` rather than silently dropping interaction.
7. Route Hero, Overview, Workspace, Scenarios, Globe, FX, and Reports through shared candidate-selection and status presentation helpers.

Acceptance rerun should require all 1,336 rows (or the new current-fingerprint count) to reconcile, zero failed-eligibility priced rows, zero hidden adjustments, complete multi-territory identity, and exact hover/click structure equality.

## 17. Already correct — do not reopen

- Current-fingerprint selection and `canonical-1.52.0` engine pinning are correct; do not replace them with newest-row heuristics.
- Source declared gross remains canonical and scenario-independent; do not force Little Utopia's immaterial $2 leaf variance into a redistributed budget.
- Incentive ≤ served QPE for every priced structure; current dollar-cap application has zero breach.
- Unpriced structures correctly carry null incentive/NPC.
- Comparability is separate from priceability; do not rank relocation/component rows before normalization is comparable.
- Manitoba current display/geographic code is correct and distinct from Cyprus; do not add a Manitoba-specific patch.
- US/Canada `globeKey` admin-1 preservation is correct in principle.
- Globe hit targets that exist have adequate CSS pointer/keyboard wiring and z-order; do not rewrite raycasting.
- Frontend screens render backend incentive/NPC fields rather than recomputing economics.
- The prior summed conditional-fund cap is no longer displayed as project incentive.
